*I duck down and underneath the rail. After a moment, Marina does the same.*
*We’re officially out of bounds.*
*Across the small field, I can still just barely make out the shape of the ruins. They sit close to the mouth of a large cave that runs up the mountain like a scar.*
Amber: "Okay, come on."
*There’s an incline, but it’s not too bad. Next to me, Marina spreads her arms out for balance, but I’m confident enough to not need to.*
*Crossing over to the ruins themselves will be the riskiest part. There’s no cover, and anyone who happens to look over will see us.*
*We just have to hope that the tour group has moved to somewhere without a line of sight on us.*
Amber: "Okay, hurry."
*I grab Marina’s hand again and start to run.*
*She struggles to keep up, so I slow down a little bit. It takes maybe two minutes from one end to the other.*
*When we reach the far wall, Marina’s breathing is strained.*
Amber: "Don’t do much running, do you?"
*Every word is punctuated with a pant.*
*I’m winded too, but fight my lungs to not show it. I have to look strong for Marina.*
Amber: "Ready for the fun part?"
Marina: "Is it actually fun?"
Amber: "Not yet. We’ve still gotta get up there."
*I point to the lip of the cave, a good fifty feet or so above us still.*
*Marina’s face goes pale as she stares at the sheer rock wall in front of us.*
Marina: "We’re not gonna have to climb that, are we?"
Amber: "How else?"
*I wait a second for the shock to hit her face before I laugh and shake my head.*
Amber: "Just kidding. Come over here."
*A short distance away is a slope, somewhat steep but not too long. It leads up into part of the cave close to the ruins.*
Amber: "We actually do have to go up this one."
*Her face still looks doubtful.*
Marina: "I dunno if I can get up that."
Amber: "Sure you can. It’s easy; look."
*I hike up a couple steps. Dirt and a bit of gravel tumble down past my feet, but it’s mostly stable.*
Amber: "It’s almost all dirt. If it was loose rock we’d be in trouble."
*Still looking uncertain, Marina takes a careful step up behind me. Then another.*
*She slides back down a little bit with each step.*
Amber: "Try to spread your weight out a little more evenly. You don’t wanna lose your balance and fall over."
*By doing what I say, she’s able to reach where I’m standing.*
Amber: "Good job. Now just do that like fifty more times."
*Marina groans, but I’ve already started.*
*I glance back behind me. She’s trailing only a couple steps back. For all her worrying, she’s doing okay.*
*I’m just about to compliment her when I feel the earth shift beneath me and my foot slides loose.*
*With a yelp, I lose my balance, arms flailing as I try not to fall.*
*Then, faster than I can process, Marina’s arm shoots out and grabs my sleeve. She tugs me back upright.*
Marina: "Are you okay? Oh my God, is your ankle alright? It’s not broken is it?"
*Panic is clearly painted across her face.*
Amber: "No, I’m fine, I’m fine."
*The only thing injured is my pride.*
Marina: "Are you sure?"
Amber: "Yeah, thanks. Good save."
*We climb up the rest of the way together, but slowly.*
*At the top, both of us stretch and catch our breath.*
Amber: "That wasn’t so bad, was it?"
Marina: "Besides the part where you almost died, maybe?"
Amber: "Pfft, I didn’t almost die. Besides, that’s what I have my sidekick for."
*She gives me a playful shove.*
Marina: "Hey! I told you I’m not the sidekick here."
Amber: "And you can do that by finding this thing before I do."
*Now that we’re actually up in the ruins themselves, it’s more clear just how tough this is going to be. The cave stretches a long way, like a deep scar in the mountain.*
*Delicate clay buildings, some barely as tall as we are, are arranged throughout.*
*They say this was a village, but it’s hard to imagine anyone living here. Everything is so cramped and small.*
Marina: "Where do you think we should start?"
Marina: "What if someone already found it? Like, a long time ago."
*I shrug, but that’s probably impossible to see. The sun has now gone entirely down, leaving us in shadowed darkness.*
*I wonder why Marina doesn’t seem bothered by it this time.*
Amber: "If they did, then I haven’t heard anything about it. We’ve just gotta start looking. Try to be careful though."
Marina: "I feel bad being here… Maybe this was a bad idea."
Amber: "Be super careful, then."
Amber: "And watch for spiders."
*I flick on the spare flashlight and pass it to her. Just in time to see the look of disgust at the mention of spiders.*
*I worry that our lights might be visible from a distance, even though they aren’t very powerful.*
*The only alternative would be sneaking over in broad daylight, and we’d be much more likely to be caught then.*
*I’m not finding anything except dirt. I brush my fingers along the clay bricks in hopes of finding a loose stone or secret compartment, but there's nothing like that.*
*Nearby, Marina’s doing the same thing. She’s hitched up her dress so that it doesn’t drag on the ground, and I force myself to look away, rather than stare.*
*She offers quite a view, though.*
*After a moment, Marina giggles. I’m not sure why.*
*I haven’t spoken at all, because I don’t want our voices to echo in the cave and give us away. But silently searching gets tedious fast, so I decide to try whispering.*
Amber: "What’s so funny?"
Marina: "I dunno. Everything, I guess. I was just thinking how weird this is."
Marina: "Like, it’s just so random, you know?"
Amber: "Alright, Aristotle."
Marina: "Who’s that?"
Amber: "Never mind."
Amber: "But yeah. It is kinda crazy."
*Deciding that the building I was scouring is a lost cause, I move on to the next one.*
Marina: "I know it’s probably not a big deal for you since you do this stuff all the time, but this is honestly the most fun I’ve ever had. I think."
Amber: "Destroying ancient Native American landmarks?"
Marina: "I’m being careful! Super careful! But just in general. Being out here on the road."
Marina: "I thought maybe I was making a mistake when I left but not anymore."
Amber: "I’m glad."
*And I am. I really am.*
Amber: "Guess it worked out well for both of us."
*I can’t see her from here, but I know she’s smiling.*
*Then she giggles again.*
Marina: "Aw, Amber, you’re a big ol’ softie after all, aren’t you?"
Amber: "Am not!"
Amber: "I take it back, I should’ve left you on the side of the road."
*This time we both laugh. After that, we lapse back into near-silence. Just the sound of us crawling around, or occasionally moving to another building to search.*
*Soon, the happy atmosphere starts to dissolve as we start getting frustrated.*
*Or at least I do.*
*It takes maybe 20 minutes before we’ve looked over all the buildings. There aren’t too many, and combing over them isn’t that hard.*
*But we don't find anything.*
*Not even a clue or hint that we’re in the right spot.*
*Sighing through my nose, I start double-checking some of the larger homes.*
*All the crawling and bending is starting to take a toll on me. When I straighten up, my back is sore, and sweat presses my hair to my forehead.*
*We should’ve brought some bottles of water with us.*
*I lean against a rock wall and massage my shoulders. Marina finishes up a building, then joins me.*
*She’s biting her lip, frowning a little.*
Marina: "Any luck?"
Amber: "Nope, nothing. You?"
Marina: "Uh uh."
*We both pause, silent.*
Marina: "Soooo..."
Marina: "That’s it, right? Like, we’ve checked all the buildings."
Amber: "Can't be."
Amber: "We must have missed something. I’m going to try checking out the cave itself."
*She just stands there.*
*Watches as I head over to the far side.*
*I shine my flashlight up and down and up again, examining every inch of the rock.*
*There's gotta be a crevice where the gold is stashed.*
*Or some other hiding place.*
*There has to be.*
*But none of the cracks are wide enough or deep enough for something like that.*
*A few minutes later and I pass by Marina, still in the same place.*
*I check her face as I go. Her eyebrows are furrowed, and she looks worried.*
*I keep hunting.*
*Images of Mariah triumphantly holding up the gold spur me on.*
*Just the thought of her smug grin pisses me off.*
*When I reach the other other end of the cave, I double back and check it again.*
*And again.*
*Finally, Marina pulls away from the wall. I hear it, but don't see it.*
Marina: "Amber, come on."
Marina: "There's no way."
*Her voice is a knife in my heart.*
*It hurts because I know she's right.*
*I sigh and flick off my flashlight.*
*Standing there, I look at the invisible ground and have to admit the truth to myself - it’s not here.*
*There’s no treasure to be found in the Mummy Cave Ruins.*
*Either we got it wrong, or someone beat us to it. Whichever the answer, there’s nowhere else in here that it could be.*
*I make my way back to Marina, slowly.*
*I can feel stinging tears of frustration beginning to well up in my eyes, which surprises me.*
*I'm sure Mariah would be laughing at me if she was here right now.*
*Abruptly, I stop walking towards her and spin around. I don’t want Marina to see.*
*There’s the sound of crunching dirt as she walks over.*
Marina: "Amber?"
*There’s worry in her voice.*
*I shake my head. I don’t know if she can see.*
*She lays her hand on my shoulder, and two tears manage to leak out of my eyes. I shake my head again.*
Marina: "Amber? Amber, it’s okay."
Amber: "No, I’m sorry. I screwed up. I was wrong."
Amber: "There’s nothing here."
Marina: "I know there isn’t. Seriously, that’s okay."
*I sob, not even knowing why.*
*Dammit. Dammit.*
*Get a grip, Amber. I wrench my eyes shut, before the waterworks really start. It burns, but I manage to hold back.*
*Taking deep breaths, I get myself under control.*
*I sink down to lean against the wall of the cave. Marina does the same.*
*Our clothes are dusty from being on the ground so much.*
*Marina pokes me in the side.*
Marina: "You okay?"
*I draw a rattly breath.*
Marina: "Yeah, I could tell."
Amber: "Can it."
*I flick her, gently, on the knee.*
Amber: "But uh, that was kind of embarrassing."
Marina: "No, it’s okay! Really. I cry all the time at home, don’t worry."
Amber: "It’s just… It’s like losing. Not finding the treasure, I mean. So I’m mad for wasting all our time on this."
Amber: "Aren't you?"
Marina: "No, why would I be?"
*She sounds genuinely surprised.*
Amber: "Because now we’re behind on the next one, too. What if someone beats us to all the rest of them?"
Marina: "Now you sound like me."
Amber: "Oh God, is there a cure?"
Marina: "Meanie."
Marina: "But really."
Marina: "I had fun, so I don’t think it was a waste of time."
Marina: "Even if we’re not like, super rich now. I’m glad we came here."
*Once again, I don’t even have to look to know she’s smiling. She’s always smiling, after all.*
*It’s one of the cuter things about her, and there are lots of those.*
*But I’m afraid to look up and meet her eyes. Afraid because of what I’ve been starting to feel lately, and how I’ve been thinking of her.*
*It’s stupid to get bothered by this now, I know. We’ve been together all day.*
*But right now, when I’m already being over-emotional, I feel like it’d be a bad idea.*
Marina: "Amber?"
*And then, by reflex, I do it anyway.*
*I look over, and I was right. My heart flutters a little. But the smile I was anticipating has been replaced by yet another look of concern.*
Amber: "Yeah, you’re right. I guess it wasn’t so bad."
*She brightens up immediately.*
Marina: "For real?"
Amber: "Mhm. I’m good now. Still, sorry for freaking out there for a sec."
Marina: "It’s okay. Don’t feel bad."
*Groaning, I stand up again. I’m definitely gonna be sore tomorrow.*
Amber: "We should get out of here though. Longer we spend, the more likely we are to get caught."
*It’s been probably 45 minutes or so since we ditched the tour group. Hopefully they didn’t get search and rescue out looking for us.*
*I offer my hand to Marina, who’s still sitting on the ground. She takes it, and I haul her to her feet.*
*After dusting herself off, we both walk to the edge of the cave. It’s impossible to see anything. Even the trail across the way is invisible from here.*
Amber: "Watch your step going down the hill."
Marina: "Yeah, we don’t want anyone to almost die again."
*Going down the hill is easier than going up, though we still have to be careful. We make it to the bottom without incident.*
*We don’t risk the flashlights until we’re back across the fence and on the trail. Now that we’re not out of bounds, it doesn’t matter if people see us.*
Marina: "Where do we go now?"
Amber: "Hm."
*I think about it for a moment. There’s not much point in catching up with the tour again. Besides, it’s probably ended by now.*
*Still, if they did notice that we disappeared, then it could be bad for us to just leave the park.*
*The last thing I want to see is our faces on 'Missing Person' posters the next time we stop at a gas station.*
*But they’ve got a 45 minute lead on us, and that’ll only grow if we were to try to find them.*
Amber: "We’ll just go back to the parking lot and get outta here. If anyone asks, we can just say we left early."
Marina: "Thank God. I was hoping you’d say that."
Amber: "How come?"
Amber: "Same. My back’s gonna hurt for a year."
Marina: "At least you have the comfy bed!"
Amber: "Hey, you could always…"
Amber: "Uh, grab some more pillows from the closet if you need."
Marina: "There are more?"
Amber: "Oh yeah. Gramps liked to have a zillion pillows when he slept."
Marina: "Oh."
*She sounds let down. Oops, maybe I shouldn’t have made that connection there.*
Amber: "Don’t worry, they weren’t like… His special pillows or anything. You can use them."
Marina: "Alright…"
*Damn, she really starts to worry when I mention him. Guess there’s good reason for that.*
*But right now, though the thought is a familiar jab of sadness, I’m not about to break down. I’m doing okay for now.*
*We’re quiet the rest of the way down the trail, though. Without having to stick to the slow pace of a guided tour, it’s actually a quick walk.*
*There aren’t any other hikers out, which surprises me. It’s dark, but not that late.*
*As we reach the parking lot, it seems like we had perfect timing. Or maybe perfectly awful.*
*Spilling out of a trailhead on the other side is the tour group that we were briefly part of. I recognize the tour guide, as well as…*
Linda: "Oh, there you girls are!"
*Linda.*
*She came from my blind spot, before we had time to hide.*
*I don’t even try to force a smile as I turn to face her.*
*She strolls up to us, Dino still asleep. Howard’s nowhere to be seen, though. Maybe he’s in their car.*
Linda: "I was hopin’ to see y’all before ya left."
Amber: "Yeah, we were pretty tired so we came back early."
Linda: "Oh, I’ll bet you are!"
*She winks at me, like she’s in on the joke. Problem is, I don’t know what the joke’s supposed to be.*
Amber: "Uh, what do you mean?"
Linda: "Don’t think I didn’t notice you two running off, now! Figured you were, ya know."
*She laughs and shakes her head.*
Linda: "I think that’s good though! Reminds me of me and my Howard when we were younger. We used to sneak off every chance we got, if ya know what I mean!"
*My face starts to turn red as I realize what she’s thinking. Marina’s probably tomato-colored, though I don’t turn to look.*
Amber: "We weren’t… Um. I mean, we’re not-"
Linda: "No need to be embarrassed or anything like that. And besides! At least ya won’t get pregnant!"
*She laughs heartily, the baby on her chest bouncing with the motion.*
Linda: "Sorry though, I’m gettin’ to be a bit too nosy for my own good. Y’all girls be safe out there. It was nice to get to know ya."
*She grabs my hand from my side and pumps it in a vigorous handshake, then does the same to Marina. Neither of us react, still dumbfounded.*
*Then she heads off towards the sea of cars. More of a puddle now, since most have already left.*
*I turn to Marina. As expected, her face is scarlet.*
Marina: "Um. Wow."
Amber: "Right? That poor baby."
Amber: "And that poor husband."
Marina: "I didn’t think people like that were real. Like, that they only existed in books and movies and stuff."
*I shrug, and start walking back towards the RV. I’m glad that Marina doesn’t push the topic of Linda’s… misconception.*
*I unlock the door and let Marina in first. We’re one of the last ones here.*
*Before I pull the door shut, I take a final look around Canyon de Chelly. Or what little of it I can see.*
*It may have been a bust, but like Marina said…*
*It wasn’t a total waste of time.*
*Inside the RV once again, I crash down into the driver’s seat and kick my feet up on the dash. It’s a relief to sit on something soft.*
*Like a satellite, Marina hovers around me, expectant.*
Amber: "So, that was a failure. Obviously."
Marina: "Do you think it might be in a different part of the canyon?"
Amber: "Maybe, but we’re not gonna find out. We could spend days out here and not find anything."
Amber: "Most likely, someone else found it before us. Maybe even before this whole treasure hunt thing started."
*She stares at the ground, then perks back up.*
Marina: "That's okay, though! All we can do is move on."
Marina: "C'mon. Show me why the Human Travel Guide is the best."
*I look at her for a moment, then with a smile, gently take it from her.*
Amber: "Sure thing."
Amber: "But that's the first and only time you're ever gonna call me that, okay?"
Marina: "'Kaaaaaaay."
*I flip through the journal and skim the pages. I skipped one of the entries last time because it looked like a jumbled puzzle. But if we're going to find the rest of the treasure, then we need to tackle this head on.*
*Bracing myself for the confusion, I find the entry and groan.*
Amber: "Oh no."
Marina: "Oh no? Does that mean you don't know where it's at?"
Amber: "The opposite. I know exactly where it's at."
Marina: "Then what's the problem?"
*I lean back in the chair and look at the ceiling.*
Amber: "It's in Arches National Park."
Marina: "Cool! Then let's go!"
*I ignore her and keep looking at the ceiling.*
Amber: "Arches has over two-thousand natural stone arches. The miner said he hid it between two stones."
*She blinks. I sigh.*
Amber: "An arch is a rock formation that curves in the air. Think of it like an upside down U."
*Finally she gets the message. Her face morphs into a blank canvas and she stares out the windshield.*
Marina: "Oh no."
Amber: "Yeah."
Amber: "If we wanna find this thing, then we need to look between every single arch in Arches."
*With a shakey smile, Marina looks back at me and tries blowing it off.*
Marina: "A-Arches National Park, huh? It's somewhere in Moab, right? You ever been?"
*With a shakey grin of my own, I respond, totally ignoring the impossible feat ahead of us.*
Amber: "Y-Yeah. I went to Moab a long time ago. Arches is really famous. Like, world famous."
*We're both sweating, staring at the long road ahead as we try our best to make casual conversation. It's like a game of chicken. Who's going to break first?*
Marina: "I’ve always wanted to go."
Amber: "Where have you vacationed to before?"
Marina: "Honestly, nowhere. Not out of the state, at least."
Amber: "For real? Not even to visit family?"
Marina: "Nuh-uh. My grandparents live in New Mexico, too. I think only my big brother has been outside of the state."
Amber: "You got any other siblings or is it just you two?"
*She shakes her head.*
Marina: "There's nine of us. I’ve got two younger brothers, three older brothers, an older sister and two little sisters."
*I whistle.*
Amber: "Damn. That’s a big-ass Christmas card."
Marina: "Right? At some point it’s just like, "aren’t you ever gonna stop?"
Marina: "I’m almost out of fingers to count on!"
*Chuckling, I stand up and walk to the back. Marina follows behind me.*
Marina: "What about you? Do you have any siblings?"
Amber: "Nah, just me. Otherwise they’d probably be riding around this old thing with us."
*As I say that, I collapse down onto my bed, sprawling out. Sitting up front felt good, but this is heavenly.*
*The bed sinks a little as she takes a seat on its edge.*
Amber: "I can’t really imagine having a single brother or sister. Definitely not eight of them."
Amber: "But I guess they don’t all live at home?"
Marina: "Actually..."
Amber: "You're kidding."
Marina: "Only one of my brothers has moved out. It’s the worst. Like, you have to make a reservation to take a shower."
Amber: "No wonder you ran away."
Marina: "Pretty much. As soon as I finished school I was just like, "okay, time to get out of here."
Amber: "How long ago was that?"
Marina: "Just a couple months. Twoish."
Amber: "What’d you major in?"
Marina: "Huh?"
Amber: "Like, what’d you get your degree in?"
Marina: "Oh. Um. Not college. High school. I just finished high school."
Amber: "Huh?"
*Using my elbows, I prop myself up.*
Marina: "What?"
Amber: "Uh, how old are you?"
*Putting her hands on her hips, Marina puffs out her cheeks and looks offended.*
Marina: "I’ll have you know it’s very rude to ask a lady her age!"
*She deflates.*
Marina: "But I’m eighteen. Why?"
Amber: "Oh thank god."
*I let myself fall back again.*
Amber: "I was afraid you were gonna be underage and that I’d get arrested for kidnapping or something."
Marina: "No way! And besides, I told you my parents were fine with it. Well, accepted it."
Marina: "What about you? How old are you?"
Amber: "Just a year older."
Marina: "Wait, you’re only nineteen?"
*Now it’s her turn to be shocked.*
Amber: "How old did you think I was?"
Marina: "Just kidding, just kidding! I figured you were like, my age or a bit older. Two years at the most."
*I don’t look at her, staring at the wall.*
Marina: "Oh come on, don’t be mad! I was just playing!"
*All of a sudden she sounds seriously regretful. I wanna mess with her, but the puppy-dog look she gives me over her shoulder is too hard to resist.*
Amber: "Sorry, what? My hearing must be getting bad in my old age."
*She throws a pillow at me from the bed beside her, and I deflect it with my arm.*
Marina: "Good save, Granny."
*Now she leans back too, staring up at the ceiling. Her hair spreads across my leg. I don’t move it.*
*Then, I realize that all the worry and nervousness we had about Arches is gone. Somewhere along the line we just forgot about it.*
*Huh.*
*I don’t know when we reached the point in our relationship where we can be comfortable being this close to each other. But I’m glad that we have.*
*Maybe, just maybe, I’m enjoying the closeness a little too much.*
*It’s been a long time since I’ve dated someone. Gramps’ health made having a girlfriend impossible - there wasn’t time for anyone else.*
*Hell, I hardly had time for myself. Not that I cared. I’d do it all over again.*
*Still, it's nice.*
*Having someone to talk to, so it's not just me and my thoughts.*
*Just as I'm about to settle into the mood, she sits up and stretches.*
Marina: "I’m pretty sleepy. Are you going to bed soon?"
Amber: "Nah. I took a nap earlier, so I’m good. It’s only a few hours to Moab, so I’ll drive us there tonight."
Marina: "Moab, huh..."
Amber: "Well, yeah."
Amber: "Something wrong?"
Marina: "No, I was just kinda wondering…"
*She trails off.*
Amber: "Wondering…?"
Marina: "Um, how far away is the Grand Canyon?"
Amber: "From here? I dunno, three or four hours. But it’s in the total opposite direction that we’re going."
Marina: "Oh. Okay."
*The crestfallen look on her face should be illegal.*
Marina: "Sorry, I should’ve looked at the map before I asked."
*Smiling, she turns and heads for bed, disappointment clinging to her like perfume.*
*Leaning against my pillows, I watch as she slowly trods off to the back.*
*Come on now, I’m not that heartless.*
Amber: "Wait a sec."
*She freezes, mid-step.*
Amber: "I guess since I was gonna drive all night anyway, we can make a detour."
Amber: "A pretty big detour, too. So don’t blame me if all the treasure is gone by the time we’re done."
*She turns around, hope springing in her face now.*
Amber: "But I guess it is like a requirement that you see the Grand Canyon when you’re sightseeing in Arizona, so that’s what we’ll do."
Amber: "You've got a lot of vacations to make up for."
*All of the tiredness and disappointment vanishes in an instant. Like a bird taking flight, she hurries back over to where I’m just standing up.*
*A trademark Marina crash-tackle-hug knocks me onto my butt and bed again. I pat her on the back a couple times before she rises.*
Marina: "Have I ever told you you’re the best?"
Amber: "Probably. But don’t you forget it."
Marina: "I couldn’t if I tried!"
Amber: "Goof."
*I poke her in the belly as I make my way up to the front again.*
Amber: "Grab me an energy drink from the fridge, will you?"
*When she sits down in the passenger seat, she has two cans of the energy drink.*
Amber: "Thought you were going to bed?"
Marina: "No way! I’m too excited now! I’ll keep you company on the drive."
Marina: "Plus, it’s only like 8 o’clock isn’t it?"
Amber: "Beats me. Sounds right."
*I pop open the tab on my drink and take a long draught. It tastes awful, but it wakes me up like nothing else. I guess the makers cut out sweeteners and nice flavors for more stimulants.*
*To drink it faster, I hold my breath to hide the taste and chug.*
*Gasping, I crumple the empty can and toss it into a trash bag. Marina, on the other hand, sips it daintily, wincing each time.*
Marina: "People seriously drink this stuff?"
Amber: "Somehow. It works though. It’s pretty much the strongest stuff you can get without a prescription."
*After taking another sip, Marina retches and sticks her tongue out. She reminds me of a cat with fur stuck on its tongue.*
*She sets the can in the cup holder beside me.*
Marina: "Do you want mine? I can’t drink this."
Amber: "I’d have a heart attack if I drank two. Just leave it and I’ll drink it tomorrow."
*Because the only way to improve an awful energy drink is to let it get warm and flat.*
*As I start the RV, Marina says something, but it’s soft enough that I can’t hear her over the engine revving.*
Amber: "What was that? Old lady hearing failing on me again."
*Smiling, she shakes her head.*
Marina: "I was just saying ‘thanks’ again."
Amber: "Don’t thank me yet. I could fall asleep at the wheel and kill us both."
Marina: "At least I’d die happy!"
Marina: "No though, not just that. For taking me with you and doing the treasure hunt and everything."
Amber: "You must be super tired. You’re saying all sorts of weird stuff now."
Marina: "I mean it! I know it’s kind of random. I’m just really happy I met you."
*After a moment of blushing, I laugh a little under my breath.*
Amber: "Yeah, me too."
*I prevent any more cheesy conversation by carefully pulling out of the parking lot and back onto the exit road.*
*I’m definitely glad that Marina’s enjoying herself. I’m having fun, too. But talking about that sort of thing… It makes me feel all squirmy inside.*
*Like I don’t match up to what Marina thinks of me.*
*Pushing those thoughts out of my head, I focus on the road.*
*We have to keep our eyes peeled for animals out here. There are lots of stray dogs in this part of the state, and dozens of deer and antelope just waiting to race cars.*
*The road’s devoid of other vehicles, though. And since I know the exits to take, more or less, we should make pretty good time.*
*Once we get back onto the highway, Marina hoists the bucket of tapes onto her lap.*
Marina: "What do you wanna listen to?"
Amber: "Hmm…"
*I glance between the road and the bin, scanning both.*
*I settle on an album that I haven’t had the heart to listen to in a while. But it feels like tonight is the right time.*
*I fish out the tape and slide it into the player. As the first chords start up, I watch Marina’s face for a reaction.*
*It won’t have the same sentimental value to her, I know, but it’s an emotional album in general. Lots of dynamics to the music and simple but heartfelt songwriting.*
*Gramps used to try to play along, but said he couldn’t quite capture the feeling himself.*
*As I’d hoped, Marina’s quiet. Which means that she’s listening. Her cheek is pressed against the window again.*
*Her reflection, barely visible, looks wistful.*
*Good.*
*For the second time today, a growing pressure starts in my eyes. I paw at them, and the motion catches Marina’s attention.*
Marina: "You sure you’re not too tired?"
Amber: "Oh, yeah. Eyes are just a little itchy."
*Dammit. My voice shakes as I speak. But if she notices, she doesn’t say anything.*
*We may yet go off the road if I end up crying. Or I might just die of embarrassment.*
*I sniffle a couple times to keep myself under control.*
Amber: "Damn allergies."
*I don’t even know if she hears me.*
*I’m kind of tempted to turn it off, and play something else. But I want Marina to hear it.*
*It won’t mean anything special to her, I’m sure. I want to see what she thinks. To see if maybe Gramps and I were crazy, or if there was more to the music and the man playing it.*
*He was a guitarist and songwriter who just went by the name of June.*
*Died a few years ago.*
*Fighting back the nostalgia and the bubbling feelings of how much I miss Gramps is hard. I’m able to hold on, though, and keep from breaking down.*
*Forty-five minutes later, when the tape ends, Marina hits the play button again to start it over.*
*I’d say it was worth it.*
*I let it play on for hours, long after Marina’s fallen asleep beside me.*
*My everything aches. It takes me a long, lazy minute to remember why I’m sitting up, forehead plastered to the steering wheel, rather than in my bed.*
*I drove all through the night to get us to the Grand Canyon, arriving at the South Rim right around the time that I couldn’t keep my eyes open any longer.*
*We’re in a campsite, apparently, but I don’t remember anything past reaching the park’s edge and paying the entry fee. I probably shouldn’t have been driving, but we made it here alright.*
*A sour taste pervades my mouth, courtesy of the empty energy drink at my side. I groan as I clamber up and head to go brush my teeth.*
*When I exit the bathroom, Marina’s waiting at the front, all dressed and ready to go.*
Marina: "Goooooood morning."
Amber: "Morning."
*I’m still too sleepy to match her enthusiasm.*
*Undeterred, she comes forward and presses a mug into my hands.*
Marina: "I made you some coffee!"
Marina: "But it was a couple hours ago, so it’s kind of cold now."
Amber: "Thanks."
*It’s also brewed too weak, but I don’t tell her that.*
*I drain my cup in seconds and then pour myself another. That’s also finished in a few long gulps.*
Marina: "You really like coffee, huh?"
Amber: "I guess. I think I’m just addicted."
Marina: "You don’t even put any cream or sugar in it, right?"
Amber: "Nope. I always drink it black."
*She wrinkles her nose in disgust.*
Marina: "I can’t stand the taste."
Amber: "I noticed."
Amber: "Have you gone and looked around yet?"
*There are dozens if not hundreds of people milling around, and that’s just what I can see from here. Most likely treasure hunters.*
Marina: "Look around? Without you?"
Amber: "Yeah, it’s what, noon?"
Marina: "Almost. But I didn’t wanna leave without you."
*That’s sweet of her.*
Marina: "What if I got lost? You’re like my map."
*Maybe not.*
Marina: "Besides, it’s boring to go look around by myself."
Amber: "You think? I used to do it lots before we met up. Was nice to be alone and think."
*Her face falls. Seconds later, I realize my mistake and mentally kick myself.*
Amber: "Not to say I don’t like having you around! I just meant it’s not so bad being on your own for a little bit."
*She ponders for a moment.*
Marina: "Well, I don’t like being alone."
*A small frown makes me think she’s upset, but then it melts away and is replaced with her trademark smile.*
*Something about how the sunlight frames her face right then makes my heart skip a beat. I want to admire her for longer, but I think my face gives it away.*
*Soon she shifts, asking with her eyes.*
Marina: "Something wrong?"
*I shake my head to clear it.*
Amber: "Nah, sorry. Just zoned out for a second. Lemme finish this coffee and then we can go."
Marina: "Okay!"
*Marina stomps over to play-hit me on the shoulder.*
Marina: "Meanie! I do my best for you!"
Amber: "Yeah, yeah."
Amber: "There aren’t enough hours in the day for you to sleep as much you want."
*She’s just kidding, but I freeze.*
*She is joking… right?*
Marina: "Amber?"
*The jaunty tone in her voice is gone.*
Amber: "Yeah."
*I croak out a response.*
Marina: "Zone out again?"
Amber: "Something like that."
*Even I’m surprised by how off-guard that caught me.*
*It was just a simple joke, but it was a little too relevant, seconds after thinking of how pretty she looked.*
*Looks.*
*I need to stop being so weird sometimes, or that’ll scare her off more than her finding out that I might, just maybe, could possibly have a crush on her.*
Marina: "Maybe you just need more coffee."
Amber: "Probably."
*Her grin gets huge.*
Marina: "You need as much coffee as I do sleep!"
Marina: "See! We’re even!"
*I laugh as I pour myself a third cup, tilting the pot to get the dregs.*
Amber: "Maybe you’re right."
*She prances back to the front door, nose held in the air like she’s won some great victory.*
*I catch her peeking back to see if I’m watching, so I make a big show of rolling my eyes.*
*She sticks her tongue out in return.*
*When I set my cup down in the sink, Marina throws the door open and waits for me to step out.*
*For having been mostly asleep, and in the dark, it looks like I did an okay job parking.*
*By which I mean that we’re actually in one of the designated camping spaces.*
*That's more than I can say for a lot of the others here.*
*Tents cover nearly every patch of land that doesn’t have an RV on it. Some even come right up against the road itself, causing the few cars that pass by to have to hug the edge.*
*Just like back in Shiprock, this is a fiasco.*
*Squads of little kids rampage around, squirting each other (and everyone else) with water guns and just being in the way.*
*One little brat runs up to us and holds up his squirt gun like he’s going to shoot me. But the death glare that I give sends him running back in the other direction.*
Marina: "Not a fan of kids?"
Amber: "Not the rotten ones."
*Marina, on the other hand, waves at the retreating boy, and giggles as a weak burst of water hits her.*
Amber: "I dunno how you can stand it."
Marina: "They’re just kids!"
Marina: "Besides, I’m pretty used to it."
Amber: "Big family, right?"
Marina: "Yep!"
*We start to walk in the direction of the canyon itself, pausing sometimes to let cars or tour groups pass us by.*
Amber: "This is such a mess. Wonder how many of them are treasure hunters."
Marina: "Well it’s the Grand Canyon, so a lot of people are probably just regular tourists, right?"
Amber: "Yeah, probably."
Amber: "I feel bad for them. And all the other people who just wanna enjoy these parks."
Amber: "What? Where’d that come from?"
Marina: "I dunno, you’re just always complaining about there being too many people around."
*Do I really complain that much?*
*Thinking about it, I guess I do.*
Marina: "So you don't like crowds, then?"
Amber: "That’s closer, yeah."
Marina: "Kinda weird that you’re so hyped for this music thing then, don't you think? Isn’t that gonna be the biggest crowd yet?"
Amber: "Sure, but that’s different."
Marina: "Why?"
Amber: "I dunno, just is."
Amber: "What are you, five? No wonder you get along with kids so well."
*We both laugh.*
Amber: "But… I guess it’s just because everyone is there for the same thing. You have something in common with them."
Marina: "Isn’t everyone here for the same thing?"
Amber: "Yeah, but…"
*I struggle to find an explanation.*
*But she laughs it off and shrugs.*
Marina: "I’m just kidding anyway. You can’t be the only one who teases."
Marina: "You don’t like big crowds, and that’s fine!"
Amber: "What about you?"
Marina: "I think it’s fun! And besides, you get to meet all sorts of cool people."
*"Special someone," huh?*
Amber: "Maybe. Would be pretty unlikely though."
Marina: "Probably. But it’s still fun to think about."
Marina: "And speaking of odds…"
Marina: "Amber…"
*Marina points at another cluster of motorhomes down the road.*
*I follow her gaze, immediately understanding.*
*Taking up two spaces, because of size and bad parking, is a familiar titan of an RV.*
Amber: "God, no."
*But Marina’s already heading towards it.*
*My nemesis.*
*Mariah’s motorhome.*
*The door flies open, seconds before Marina reaches it.*
*Out strolls Joseph, Tess close behind.*
Joseph: "You call that a break? You’ve had boyfriends that last longer."
Tess: "Sis has never had a boyfriend."
Mariah: "Quit your moaning. You two just need more work ether."
Joseph: "You mean ‘ethics,’ but you’ve probably never heard the word."
*Mariah appears in the doorway, leaning on a shovel. I can see her about to retort, but then she spots either me or Marina, and her glare is replaced with a smug grin.*
Mariah: "Well, well, well. Look who finally caught up."
Marina: "Hey guys!"
Joseph: "Oh, hey there, ladies."
*I sigh and speed up my glacial pace. May as well get this over with.*
*Mariah picks up a crate of tools from beside her and carries them out, before shoving the crate into Joseph’s hands.*
*His knees buckle at the weight, and he sets it on the ground.*
Mariah: "Did you follow our dust all the way here?"
Amber: "I wish we’d seen it, so we could’ve stayed away."
Mariah: "Maybe you should go now and get a head start on the next piece, before we find the one here."
*She clutches her shovel like a sword and points it at me.*
*I raise my hands in surrender.*
Amber: "We already had one bust at Canyon de Chelly. This one’s all yours to screw up."
*The shovel drops a couple inches, either as a response or because her arm’s getting sore.*
Mariah: "'Canyon de Chelly?' But there's only one Grand Canyon!"
Joseph: "There's more than one canyon in the world, Mare."
Amber: "Figured it was there at the Mummy Cave Ruins, but we didn’t find anything."
Amber: "So if you wanna search all 2,000 miles of the Grand Canyon then good luck."
Mariah: "Dammit!" with hpunch
*Mariah throws down her hands and the tool-bag slips from her, plummeting onto Joseph’s foot. Hard. The crates in his hand are the next to fall, this time on to the other foot.*
*The boxes burst open, flooding the ground with heavy hammers and wrenches.*
*A gasp escapes his mouth, then silence.*
Tess: "Are you gonna get drunk and cry yourself to sleep again, Sis?"
*Somehow, through pained grunts, Joseph manages to speak.*
Joseph: "The limit is once per week."
*Mariah's eyes twitch.*
*In one smooth motion, she whirls around and clonks her companions's heads, huffs, and crosses her arms.*
*Joseph reacts like any normal human being and rubs his head to soothe his neverending pain. Tess hardly acknowledges it, wobbling back and forth like a human bobblehead.*
*I don't think I'll ever get over how creepy that girl is.*
*She clonks Joseph again for good measure. Marina and I both wince.*
*I imagine this is how it was for Howard when he first met Linda.*
Tess: "At least we’d get to see the canyon that way."
Amber: "Jesus!"
*From out of nowhere, Tess pops up at my side.*
Joseph: "Tess has been wanting to go out and see the sights, but Mariah’s too busy breaking her back for that."
Marina: "Big sisters are bossy, huh?"
*Tess nods, which prompts Marina to stoop down and hug her apathetic kindred spirit.*
Marina: "You’re not alone!"
*Marina looks up at me, eyes big.*
Mariah: "That’s property theft!"
Amber: "Sorry, only one stray at a time."
Marina: "Aww."
*Marina releases Tess and reaches out her hand as they float apart.*
Amber: "Anyway, let’s get going. If they’re gonna waste all day here we can at least leave them to it."
*But Mariah waves her shovel in Joseph’s face.*
Mariah: "Pick up our crap. We’re gonna rethink our strategy."
Joseph: "'Crap?'"
*She taps the crate that she forced on him just minutes ago.*
*Joseph covers Tess’ ears then mutters something that I can’t make out.*
Marina: "Wait, I have an idea!"
*Four pairs of eyes turn to look at her.*
Marina: "Me and Amber were about to go sightseeing. Why don’t you guys come with us?"
Amber: "Hey, we agree on something."
*But Tess’ eyes sparkle with determination, nodding as she reaches up to tug on Mariah's suit.*
Marina: "That’s one vote yes!"
*She turns to me, pleading look in her eyes, hoping to make it two yes votes.*
Joseph: "I think that sounds like a good time, too."
Joseph: "It’d be a nice break."
Mariah: "I forbid it! You’ve already had a break!"
Mariah: "We have too much to focus on. Getting distracted by dusty, old rocks is the last thing we need."
Joseph: "You always say that, but look at her!"
*He points to Tess, her face shining with interest since the word ‘sightseeing.’*
Joseph: "When did she last show interest in something? Hell, has she ever shown interest in something?"
Mariah: "We're still not going."
*An irritable groan rumbles through Joseph's throat, but he still looks as casual and relaxed as he usually does.*
Joseph: "Look, we're not always going to do what you want just because you say so."
Joseph: "We're going, and if we have to, we'll force you to come along."
*Marina laughs awkwardly.*
Marina: "It's fine, it's fine. You guys don't have to come if you don't want to."
*But it's too late for that. Mariah lets out a single, disingenuous 'ha,' and marches to Joseph.*
Mariah: "You’re gonna force me to go, huh?"
Joseph: "That’s right."
*She stares him down—or up, to be accurate. The top of her head only reaches up to his chin. Regardless, she gives him a nasty grin and repeatedly pokes his chest for daring to defy her.*
*This doesn't seem to phase him, though, as his lips curl into an ominous smile.*
Mariah: "And how exactly do you plan to do that?"
*Vast and majestic, the canyon dominates the horizon, as far as my eyes can see. Calico rocks are given a murky tint by the dying sunlight.*
*It’s a breathtaking view. I just stare out over the abyss, watching as the sun slowly climbs down the sky.*
Mariah: "What are you even looking at."
*She says it flatly, not like a question. Mariah’s been this way for hours now, since Joseph practically dragged her out of the RV.*
*While the other three have amused themselves with disposable cameras and curiosity, Mariah’s tailed me like a mangy dog.*
*She won’t let me forget about her relentless apathy, punctuating it with frequent sighs.*
Amber: "I’m looking at the cliffs. And considering sending you to get a closer look at the bottom."
Mariah: "This is your fault, you know."
Amber: "What is?"
Mariah: "That we’re here. Or at least that I’m here."
Amber: "Hey, I didn’t want you along either. That was all Marina."
Mariah: "Just tell that airhead ‘no’ sometime. Guessing you’ve never turned down a request of hers in your life."
*I shoot her a dirty look at the word ‘airhead.’*
*Mariah rolls her eyes. She doesn't care one bit.*
Mariah: "Joe does it all the time."
*Her ability to miss the point is actually kind of impressive.*
Amber: "Besides, you should see the look I get if I try to stop her from something. Maybe I can’t say no."
Mariah: "Be more like me. Mental fortitude and training."
Amber: "Is almost breaking down when someone threatens to take away the key to your liquor cabinet part of that 'mental fortitude and training?'"
*She clicks her tongue in annoyance and grumbles.*
Mariah: "I could have still said no. I was just doing it for Tess."
*I turn my attention over to the impromptu photoshoot again.*
*Both the girls climb on the rocks and lean against the railings to take pictures. Joseph trails close behind, probably to make sure nobody slips. Sometimes, he’s pulled in for a picture too.*
*Marina’s taken pictures of just about everything. The sky, the ground, a pouting Mariah. As a photographer, she’s not very picky.*
*Tess, on the other hand, seems to have an actual knack for it. She’s taken pictures of scenic valleys and closeups of beautiful desert flowers in full bloom, usually with Joseph as the model.*
*She even managed to get close enough to take one of an eagle perched on a rock.*
*Not hard when you’re practically invisible, though.*
Mariah: "I don’t even know why she’s so interested in this stuff."
Mariah: "That’s a fancy way of saying 'overgrown rocks.'"
Amber: "Who cares? Shouldn’t you be glad that she is?"
Mariah: "Why? I’d prefer if she cared about something that matters."
Amber: "Like getting rich?"
Mariah: "Exactly."
Mariah: "'Don’t mess with Texas' shirts and old rocking chairs that some hicks farted on?"
Amber: "Do you ever listen to yourself talk? Just be happy she’s interested in culture and expanding her horizons."
Mariah: "Culture? Hah! Our favorite foods are bastardized versions of foreign cuisine."
Mariah: "Culture’s a sham."
Joseph: "Years later and nothing’s changed."
*Since the other girls are taking a break, Joseph has joined us.*
Joseph: "They wanna take some pictures with you."
Joseph: "Your sister wanted me to ask."
*Her mouth shuts, and she rolls her eyes yet again.*
Mariah: "Fine."
*She stalks over to where they are, kicking up clouds of dust with each heavy step.*
Joseph: "Sorry if she's causing you trouble. She can be a bit of a handful."
Joseph: "I know it’s hard to believe, but there's a good heart in there... somewhere."
Amber: "She’d probably sell it for a dollar if she could."
*Joseph laughs heartily.*
Joseph: "You might be right."
Joseph: "That’s just how she is, I guess. Been that way since we were kids."
*It's hard to imagine that someone could not only know Mariah as a child, but want to maintain a friendship with her for the rest of their lives.*
*Come to think of it, Joseph’s a bit of an odd case compared to the other two. Odd just because he’s so normal.*
Amber: "Hey, can I ask you something?"
Joseph: "Shoot."
Amber: "Do you care about the treasure?"
*He scratches the stubble on his chin and thinks about it.*
Amber: "Like, you have a good head on your shoulders. Why do you let her drag you around?"
Joseph: "Well, it's not that I don't care about the treasure. Being rich doesn’t sound like a bad gig at all."
Joseph: "But I wouldn't say I have a good head on my shoulders, either. If it came down to treasure hunting or kicking back on a couch all day, I’d pick the couch."
Amber: "Figures."
*His own broad smile returns. I didn’t even notice it disappear.*
Joseph: "But as for them."
Joseph: "One of them needs someone to look out for her, and the other is ten."
*Now we both laugh.*
Joseph: "If she’s the one who gets me off the couch, I’m the one who gets her to sit down sometimes."
Joseph: "Even back in school, she was always picking fights. Didn’t matter if it was with another kid or a teacher."
Joseph: "Got her ass kicked a couple times. If she were all on her own, she’d tear apart the whole Southwest."
Joseph: "That make sense?"
*I look over at where the three of them are still taking pictures. Tess is standing on a rock so that she’s at the same level as the others.*
*Right now, two of her fingers hold Mariah’s mouth into the shape of a smile. On Mariah’s other side, Marina holds the camera out and takes their picture.*
*Then Tess takes one, though Marina keeps her hand out of Mariah’s mouth.*
Amber: "Yeah, I know what you mean."
Joseph: "Plus, someone’s gotta make sure Tess doesn’t starve."
*Catching us watching, Marina waves vigorously. She nearly knocks Mariah’s lights out while she does. I wave back.*
*Tess says something that gets Marina’s attention, and after a second Marina hurries over. I wince as I watch her clumsily climb over the rocks, scared that she’s going to slip and hurt herself.*
*But she arrives unharmed.*
Marina: "Tess wanted me to ask if you’d go be in the pictures too, Joe. She says she wants all three of you."
Joseph: "Looks like that’s my cue."
*With nothing more to say, he drifts over to the others and exchanges spots with Marina.*
*Tess passes him the camera, and he holds it up to his eye.*
Joseph: "Why are you using this cheap thing anyway? The camera on Mariah's phone is way better."
*He takes the camera from Tess and dangles it from his fingers, observing it like it's an ancient, long buried artifact.*
Tess: "The memory is full from all her journal entries."
Amber: "Journal?"
Tess: "It's more like angsty poetry."
*Mariah turns toward me, panic and fury in her eyes, but she’s too slow.*
Joseph: "How did that one go again?"
Joseph: "Oh yeah!"
*Mariah slouches.*
Mariah: "I hate you both."
Joseph: "Aww, but we love you, Mariah."
*Tess clings to her sister’s waist like a magnet, showing her affection.*
*It goes ignored.*
Joseph: "Now squeeze in, I'm not sure how we're all gonna fit."
*We watch for a moment, before I tap Marina on the shoulder.*
*That should keep them busy for a while.*
Amber: "Quick! While they’re distracted!"
*I grab Marina’s hand and dash off, dragging her behind me, Her feet have trouble keeping up, but I don’t slow down. I don’t look back.*
Amber: "Just keep running."
*We wander around for a while until we find another viewpoint that's empty of other people. I’m guessing that a lot of the treasure hunters are out looking now, since the oppressive heat isn’t as bad.*
*I lean against the rail, my head propped up on my arms. The cool metal feels nice against my skin. Marina does the same. She sighs wistfully.*
Marina: "So pretty…"
Amber: "Which part?"
Marina: "All of it! Especially the sunset."
*Nearly all the sunlight has been swallowed by the canyon’s colossal darkness. Shadows cast by the stones mute the gorgeous colors as the sun melts into the tip of the canyon.*
*It’s barren and desolate, but Marina’s right—it’s pretty.*
Marina: "Oh! Let’s take a picture! Before it gets too dark!"
Amber: "Uhh…."
Marina: "You’re not getting out of this one! You haven’t been in any of the pictures today."
Amber: "Yeah, that’s on purpose."
Marina: "Scooch in."
Amber: "Like, together?"
Marina: "Duh!"
*She slides towards me, and I squirm away on instinct.*
Marina: "What’s wrong?"
*Immediately, before I can answer, she grins slyly.*
Marina: "Waaaaaaaaaaait a second."
Marina: "You’re camera-shy, aren’t you?"
Amber: "Am not!"
*I retort a little too quickly.*
Marina: "Oh?"
*Clearly, she doesn’t believe me.*
Amber: "It’s not that I’m afraid of them or anything, I just don’t like having my picture taken."
Marina: "How come?"
*I shrug.*
Amber: "Just don’t."
Marina: "Well, you’ll have to deal with it this time!"
*She zips over and, before I can react, has pressed her cheek to mine.*
*The shutter clicks and I’m momentarily stunned by the flash.*
*Before I can protest or enjoy the closeness, she’s already pulled away and is checking the results.*
Marina: "It’s cute! Look!"
*She shoves the camera into my face, but it’s so close and the screen is so small that I can’t actually see the picture.*
*I just nod.*
Marina: "I can’t wait to get these developed!"
*Turning to me, Marina pauses.*
Marina: "How do you get photos developed, anyway?"
Amber: "We’ll have to take them to a store and have them do it. Most big supermarkets can."
Marina: "Okay! First thing tomorrow then!"
Amber: "Sure."
*Like a hummingbird, Marina darts forward and gives me a tight hug.*
Marina: "Thanks for coming out here. It’s been so amazing. I hope it was worth it."
*I look down at her, pressed against me.*
Amber: "Yeah, it was."
*Marina pulls away, to my great disappointment. She opens her mouth like she’s going to say something, but then seems to change her mind.*
Amber: "What?"
Marina: "What what?"
Amber: "You were about to say something. What was it?"
Amber: "Hey, you can’t do that. Now I really wanna know!"
*She giggles, but it’s distracted-sounding.*
Marina: "Okay, fine."
Marina: "I was just gonna ask, why is it that you hate Mariah and her group so much?"
Amber: "Where’d that come from?"
*Marina turns away and stares out over the canyon again. It’s getting pretty dark. She’s quiet for a moment before she talks.*
Marina: "I dunno. You just complain about them a lot."
Amber: "I don’t hate them, dummy."
Amber: "Joe and Tess are fine. It’s just Mariah I have a problem with."
Marina: "How come?"
Amber: "She’s a bitch. Loud and rude and obnoxious. I really hate people like that."
Amber: "She thinks she’s always right, too."
Marina: "Sounds like someone else I know."
Amber: "Don’t give me that! We’re nothing alike!"
*Marina laughs.*
Marina: "Yeah, yeah."
*Half a minute passes in silence.*
Marina: "You’re way cuter than she is."
*Time stops, and so does my heart.*
*Everything feels like it’s frozen.*
*I look over at Marina, but she’s looking at the canyon, not me.*
*Still just a couple inches away.*
*I gulp, swallowing the lump forming in my throat.*
Amber: "You said that before, I think. You’re cuter."
Marina: "Oh hush! I’m trying to compliment you."
*I fall silent for a moment, not sure of how to respond.*
Amber: "You know, back when we first met, I meant what I said about you being cute, too."
*She giggles again.*
Marina: "I’d forgotten about that."
*As if on cue, a light breeze starts up, causing her hair to billow out like a cloud. Her sidetail, hastily bound, looks as if it might come undone.*
*Finally, she turns to look at me, her eyes wide and lips glistening.*
*My palms are dry and my knees tremble.*
*Gulping yet again, I cross the short distance between us in a half-step, leaning in and-*
Mariah: "There you are!"
*Someone’s about to die.*
*Mariah’s shrill voice cuts through the moment, rupturing my eardrums and patience. I pull away, and Marina has already turned around to face them.*
*The trio appear like specters out of the darkness.*
Mariah: "You left us behind!"
Amber: "We tried to, at least."
*Cowed into submission, Marina’s shoulders fall.*
Amber: "Back off. I wanted to go see the sunset without you sighing about it."
Mariah: "Whatever. It was a waste of time either way."
Mariah: "Come on, we’re going back."
*Mariah lopes off down the path at a brisk pace. Marina and Tess are back to photographing anything and everything, though the low light means that the pictures won’t show up.*
*Joseph and I trail behind at the end of the party.*
Joseph: "How was the sunset?"
*Sighing, I pull ahead. Right now, even Joseph seems like a little too much.*
Amber: "It was okay."
*The campsite has cleared up a lot since we set out this morning. It’s still pretty full, but at least it's not claustrophobic anymore.*
*A few campfires illuminate people that are left behind. Plus, the smoke keeps the bugs away.*
*All five of us huddle outside my RV. The goodbyes are taking a while, since Marina has to hug everyone at least twice.*
*Then she says something to reignite the conversation and the whole process starts over.*
*Finally, I grab the door handle to signal that we’re about done. Marina automatically begins the final round of hugs.*
Marina: "So, what’s next for you guys?"
*Maybe not.*
Mariah: "Back to poring over the journal to see what we can find. Once we locate it, it’s straight off to wherever."
*Joseph heaves a heavy sigh.*
Joseph: "No rest for the weary."
Mariah: "You don’t get weary, slacker. You get motivated."
Marina: "Oooh. You should put that on a shirt."
Mariah: "Would you buy one?"
Tess: "Nobody would."
Mariah: "Then no."
*All of us, even Mariah, laugh a little.*
*Finally, Joseph puts his arm around Tess and starts to lead them away.*
*Starting down the path, they walk towards their motorhome.*
Amber: "Wait a sec!"
*They freeze.*
*Marina looks at me too, curious.*
Amber: "Next piece is almost definitely at Arches, in Utah."
*I hear Marina gasp, though I don’t see it.*
*Joseph and Tess both look to Mariah, their profiles barely visible from the flickering campfires in the distance.*
*Mariah nods stiffly.*
Mariah: "Thanks."
*It’s probably the first time she’s ever said that word.*
*With a wave of her hand, she turns and they finally leave.*
*Marina and I both watch until they vanish out of sight. Then Marina squeals.*
Marina: "I’m so proud of you, Amber! And shocked! That was super nice of you!"
Amber: "Wasn’t much. This one’s pretty easy to figure out when you take the time. I probably saved them half an hour at best."
Marina: "Still. It’s the thought that counts!"
Marina: "Why’d you do it, though?"
Amber: "Someone’s gotta take care of them, after all."
*I look away, pretending to scratch an itch on my cheek.*
Amber: "They're not that bad."
*The solemn loneliness of the open road extends far past my headlights and into the dark.*
*A cool wind breezes through the cracked window and tosses around our hair as a jazzy experimental track crackles through the speakers. With each horn screech, Marina sinks further into her seat.*
*She looks miserable.*
Amber: "Do you want me to turn down the music a little?"
Marina: "Oh no, I’m fine. It’s not bothering me."
*She tries to disguise the glint of agony in her eyes by speaking in her usual peppy tone, but the pain still seeps through the cracks in her voice.*
*A hollowness anchors down my chest. I don’t try burying it. The last time someone told me everything was fine I ended up losing them. Maybe I’m just paranoid, but I don’t want to risk it.*
Amber: "Seriously, are you okay?"
Amber: "Don’t lie to me."
*My sharpness takes her off guard and she shifts her gaze. But I don’t care. This could be urgent.*
*Sighing, she hangs her head.*
Marina: "Well…"
Amber: "Yeah?"
Marina: "I kind of need to…"
*Her voice trails off into a pit. Regret and shame color her face, as if she’s brought great shame to her household or something equally dumb.*
*My voice grows tense—urgent, my driving sloppy. Every pore of my clammy palms is stuck to the wheel. The hollowness comes back.*
Amber: "Marina, what is it?"
Marina: "I kind of need to, you know…"
*Marina winces, her mouth stretching into a pallid grimace. She clutches at her stomach and groans.*
*You’re kidding.*
*I slap my hand against my forehead.*
*How many more times am I going to let myself get worked up over something so stupid?*
Amber: "What did I tell you about that burrito?"
Marina: "I know! But I was so hungry!"
Amber: "You never trust gas station burritos, Mare! That’s one of the first rules of the road."
Marina: "I didn’t think it would end up like this! I didn’t knooow!"
*A flood is starting to form in her eyes. Whether it’s because of pain or my scolding, I’m not sure.*
*Hopefully it’s both and she takes this opportunity to learn something.*
Amber: "Well, you’re gonna have to deal with it for now. The septic tank needs to be drained and I can’t do that until we get to Arches."
Marina: "Wh-What?"
*Despair sets in and the hopelessness of the situation dawns on her.*
Amber: "I’ll stop if we pass a gas station or something, but that’s not gonna be for a while."
*Despair turns into desperation. Marina frantically looks for something to latch onto, anything to help reclaim her hope.*
Marina: "What about a rest stop?"
Amber: "You serious?"
Marina: "Sure, why not? I saw a sign for one down the road. Should be coming up pretty quick."
Marina: "What? And why not?"
Amber: "Because you’re better than that."
Marina: "No I’m not, I swear! Please, I really gotta go!"
Amber: "Rest stops aren’t for people like us. They’re for people like Mariah."
Amber: "Only the scum of the earth use them."
*At least, that’s what Gramps used to say.*
*I’ve never actually been to one, mostly because he refused to use them. But if a people-loving hippie like Gramps referred to them as ‘cesspools of villainy, where the seedy underbelly of humanity flocks,' then I’m not sure I want to.*
Marina: "Amber."
Marina: "I don’t care if it makes me scum. If we don’t stop now, I am going to explode."
*I wave her off, but before I can counterattack she dives in an inch from my face.*
Marina: "EXPLODE, AMBER!" with hpunch
Marina: "We are going to that rest stop, whether you like it or not. Do you hear me?"
*I gulp and reply with a shaky nod as Marina looks on with crazed eyes.*
*I never knew she could be so scary.*
*She backs off and the usual pluck in her voice returns, flipping from sour to sweet in an instant.*
Marina: "Thank yoooou."
*Defeated and mildly disturbed, I jitter my eyes back and forth and wait for the exit.*
*Who knows? Maybe it won’t be so bad.. Wouldn’t be the first time Gramps over-exaggerated. I’m ninety percent sure he was wrong about the government introducing the plague to local cat populations.*
*I pull over to an isolated exit ramp, which trails off into a dark lot.*
*The lonely island is lit by a sharp light emanating from a shack on the edge of the lot. It brightens up the immediate area, and the surrounding terrain falls off into a dim glow.*
*Despite the eerie darkness, we’re not alone. A lone big-rig with a half-assed parking job hulks over the lot.*
*Must have been in a rush.*
*Rolling my eyes, I squeeze into a parking space that isn’t being hogged by the trailer.*
*I cut the ignition, and in the ensuing silence, scan the shadowed area for anything unusual.*
*As I watch, a silhouette emerges from a door, framed by the light from within.*
*But before I can warn her, Marina springs from the motorhome to run for the shack.*
Amber: "Hey, wait! It’s not safe!"
*Without pulling the key from the dash, I launch out the door and after Marina.*
*Why the hell do we always end up running?*
*The figure towers in the entranceway.*
*Kicking up gravel, I screech to a standstill right behind Marina.*
*Marina keeps jogging in place, unable to distract herself from the inevitable any other way.*
*As my eyes adjust, his features materialize. He stares at us for an uncomfortable period of time, the bill of his unwashed baseball cap masking his eyebrows, making his stoic expression even harder to read.*
Trucker: "The woman’s bathroom is closed."
*He points to a paper taped to the door, the words ‘out of order’ scribbled in marker.*
Trucker: "I checked."
*His scratchy voice rings with a slight twang. Dried food stains sit splattered on his tank top, his flannel unable to conceal the ripe stench.*
*I have to force myself to not look away.*
Marina: "I can’t hold it anymore! What do we do what do we do what do we do what do we dooooo?"
Amber: "Relax. Just use the guys’ bathroom."
Marina: "Wh-What? Is that even legal?"
Amber: "Dunno. Probably. Just use it, I’ll keep watch."
*The man interjects, his eyes still fixated on us.*
Trucker: "Don’t worry. I wasn’t too bad this time. It should be cleared out."
*A disgusted groan slips out of me. I’m going to pretend I didn’t hear that.*
Marina: "Well, if you say so…"
*Without a second of hesitation, Marina races past the man and into the restroom.*
*Her voice echoes from within.*
*I look at the man and shrug while awkwardly laughing off Marina’s comment.*
*Why is he still here?*
Amber: "So, uh, is that your truck?"
*His serious expression morphs into a wide grin, showing off his sickly, yellow teeth, each about as cracked and decaying as the walls.*
*Wiping his nose with the back of his hand, he leans up against an old soda machine.*
Trucker: "Why yes, actually. Yes it is."
*He deepens his voice, letting the tone sway up and down.*
*I can’t tell if he’s trying to be cool, seductive, charming, or just has a case of vertigo.*
*Either way, I don’t think it has quite the effect he’s going for.*
Trucker: "Well, tech-i-nactually, the trailer belongs to the company I work for."
Trucker: "But the truck? That zero to sixty miles an hour in ten seconds? That’s all me, sweetheart."
*He looks me straight in the eyes, emphasizing sweetheart, and I struggle to keep down the bile climbing up my throat.*
Amber: "Oh, that’s nice. It’s very, uh…"
*I try to think of a compliment, something that will take the conversation somewhere else. Anywhere but there.*
Amber: "It’s very red."
*Nailed it.*
Trucker: "Red on the outside, sure. But I promise you, that interior is pure white."
*He winks.*
*If not for Marina, I would have driven off screaming into the night by now. I’m going to have to take a shower once we get to the Arches.*
Trucker: "In fact."
*He isn’t stopping? Why isn’t he stopping?*
Trucker: "Maybe you and your little friend wanna see why they call it my…"
*He slams his elbow against the soda machine. Nothing happens.*
*Cursing under his breath, he does it again with more force. This time, a can tumbles down, the momentum sending it rolling out the cache and across the asphalt.*
Trucker: "Pickup truck?"
Amber: "Marina! Are you almost done?"
Marina: "ALMOST!"
*You were right about rest stops, Gramps. Why didn’t I listen?*
Trucker: "So how about it?"
*Presented with the option to feign ignorance or bash his head against the wall, I choose to feign ignorance, but only by a smidge.*
*For Marina’s sake.*
Amber: "Golly, Mister. We really appreciate the offer, but we already have a set of wheels."
*Please let that work.*
Trucker: "Ah, I understand."
*Thank god.*
*The trucker sighs and wipes the sweat from his brow.*
Trucker: "‘Sides, I’m pretty busy, as is. After I finish this run, I’ma’ find myself some treasure."
*Oh no.*
Amber: "Treasure?"
Trucker: "Yeah, y’know, that gold that miner left lying around. It’s all over the radio."
Amber: "I may have heard a thing or two about it."
*Great, another weirdo looking for the treasure. Just what we needed.*
Trucker: "It’s on my route, so I figured why not."
*Marina prances out of the restroom, restored to her old self.*
Marina: "All done! You two have a nice chat?"
*I look at Marina, then the trucker, then to his truck.*
*Yet another despicable idea wiggles into my head. One that goes beyond picking on Mariah or leaving a tour.*
Amber: "Y’know, we had a really nice talk."
Trucker: "Really?"
*He says it like even he has trouble believing it.*
Amber: "Sure did!"
Amber: "In fact, I have to go check on something in the R.V. real quick. Why don’t you take up where I left off and talk with good ol’—"
Amber: "What’s your name?"
Trucker: "The boys on the road call me Jumbo."
Amber: "Oh god."
*To Marina’s terror, he bares his greasy smile.*
*She backs away and half-shields herself behind me, her voice softening to a whimper.*
Marina: "Amber, I don’t think I want to talk to this guy."
*I block my lips with my hand, lowering my voice to a whisper.*
Amber: "I know, but just try to keep him distracted. I won’t be long."
Amber: "I’ll be right back, you two!"
*Playing up the act, I skip across the lot and back to the motorhome.*
Marina: "Wait! Amber!"
*I fight back the invisible tears.*
*Your sacrifice is for the greater good, Marina. It won’t be in vain.*
*I swing open the R.V.’s door and rush to the nearest drawer, rummaging through until I find what I need.*
*Screwdriver. Perfect.*
*Exiting the motorhome, I sneak over to the big rig, tiptoeing so I’m not spotted.*
*The driver’s side provides sufficient cover, hiding me from the trucker and Marina.*
*I bend down and take a knee on the gravely concrete. With the screwdriver in hand, I unscrew the tire’s valve stem. Once it’s off, I take the screwdriver and insert it into the hole. The air seeps out with a steady hiss as the tire withers.*
*‘Jumbo’ and Marina’s conversation carries across the lot. Even from here, I can almost feel the spit spray from his mouth as he tries chatting up Marina, with the exact same tactics he used on me.*
Trucker: "There is one kink that there truck has, though."
*He pauses. I cringe, bracing for the incoming punchline.*
*Marina manages to squeeze out a stifled laugh, though it sounds more like uncontrollable sobbing.*
*Jumbo does the same, unleashing his crusty laugh upon the earth. It sounds like he might hack up an ashtray.*
*Keep the screaming inside, Amber. Resist the urge to repeatedly run him over with the motorhome.*
*I scurry from tire to tire, deflating them until my mission has been accomplished.*
*Sticking the loose caps in my back pocket, I emerge from the shadows and run back to Marina.*
*Just in time, too.*
Trucker: "Marina’s a pretty name. Sounds like it could bring flowin’ water and moisture to anywhere, even a barren wasteland like this."
Amber: "Alrighty, I’m back. Time to go, Marina."
*I grab her shoulders and push her away from the trucker. My sole focus now is getting us out of here.*
Trucker: "Aww, but we was havin’ so much fun."
*A cold chill shoots up my spine, causing the hairs on the back of my neck to stick up. We walk faster.*
Amber: "I’m sorry, Mr. Jumbo, but we’re in a bit of a rush, too. We appreciate the conversation, though. Ain’t that right, Marina?"
*She swings her head back and forth, mouthing ‘no’ over and over.*
Trucker: "Well, maybe we’ll meet down the road sometime."
Trucker: "Then we can have even more fun."
*He starts laughing again. Our feet move even faster than before. His laughter echos across the lot and bouncing off the nearby hills, growing louder until it becomes all I can hear.*
Amber: "Run. Don’t look back. Just run."
Marina: "I’m trying, Amber!"
*I push her harder. Our feet finally pick up enough momentum and we scramble for the motorhome, the cackling now sounding closer than ever.*
*Marina reaches the RV first and leaps inside, immediately twisting to lock her door.*
*I jump in next, without bothering to clip in my seatbelt or even check the rear.*
*The engine roars to life and I slam on the gas pedal, leaving a plume of exhaust behind as we screech onto the road.*
*When I’m sure we’re safe, I let out the biggest sigh of my life.*
*Gravity is lighter, the air conditioning is fresher. Life is a gift.*
*A hard force repeatedly hits my shoulder, shattering my zen-like appreciation of existence.*
*Marina swings one last punch. This one hits the hardest.*
Marina: "Jerk!" with hpunch
Amber: "Sheesh, that hurts."
Marina: "Good!"
Amber: "I might deserve it. Just a little, though."
Marina: "You think?"
*I rub my shoulder, trying to soothe the throbbing. She packs a surprisingly hard punch for such a tiny girl.*
*Marina flings herself back into her seat and pouts.*
Marina: "Why did you even leave, anyway?"
Amber: "He said he was after the treasure, so I decided to conduct some secret ops."
*Grinning, I dig into my back pocket and pull out the caps. It takes a few seconds for Marina to register what I’ve done, but once she does, she joins me with a goofy grin of her own.*
Marina: "You’re so sneaky, Amber."
*I toss the caps out the window and take a sip of my coffee. It’s cold now, but drinking it has never felt more satisfying.*
Marina: "But Amber."
Amber: "Hm?"
Marina: "Can we never go to a rest stop again?"
*We ended up spending the night in a department store parking lot. There were plenty of rest stops, but those are off the table—permanently.*
*Like usual, I woke up pretty early and got us on the road again. Even after waking up briefly, Marina passed back out until we were already in Moab.*
*Moab’s the quintessential "outdoorsy" town. It’s built alongside the Colorado River, winding through a canyon. Every other store sells rafting supplies or hiking gear or camping equipment.*
*Even some of the houses are made to look like log cabins and other rustic architecture.*
*You can see tall, forested mountains from any part of town, but it’s not entirely out of the desert, either.*
*It’s the time of morning where most people are on their way to work. There’s a disgustingly large amount of traffic.*
*It’s obvious why. Shovels and pickaxes stick out of truck beds and cracked windows like porcupine quills.*
*At a glance, I’d say three quarters of the people contributing to our glacial pace are prospectors. Which means I don’t have any room to complain.*
*After watching the next light change three times, but still not reaching it myself, I sigh and pull into a nearby diner.*
*At first I was just planning to grab some coffee and a bagel, and something for when Marina wakes up.*
*But with her trademark perfect timing, I hear her climb out of bed right as I turn off the RV.*
Marina: "We there? Is this Arches?"
*She stumbles up to the front still looking mostly asleep. I don’t know how she can still be tired—she must have gotten 10 hours.*
Amber: "Close. We're in Moab. Arches is still a few miles up the road."
Marina: "Oh. Why'd we stop?"
Amber: "'Cause I’m hungry. And traffic’s practically stalled. It could be lunch time before we get there."
Marina: "You were just gonna eat without me?"
*Even through her grogginess, she sounds shocked and offended.*
Amber: "I was gonna grab you something to go. But I will eat without you if you don’t get ready fast."
Marina: "Meanie."
Amber: "Yeah, yeah. Complain about me once you're dressed."
*I can see the start of a smile as she turns back to get ready. I inspect my nails while I wait.*
*A few minutes later and she hops up front like the Easter Bunny, chipper as ever.*
Amber: "It’s like your energy is stored in your outfit or something. You get dressed and wake right up."
*Marina puts on airs.*
Marina: "I don’t know what you mean. I’m always cheerful and alert."
*Her pretense is dropped in a flash.*
Marina: "Do not!"
Amber: "You do."
*I lead the way out of the RV.*
Amber: "Loud. Like an elephant seal."
*Marina blusters about for a retort while I just wait by the door, laughing.*
*She makes a "hmph" noise as she passes by me, crossing the parking lot. Still, she holds the door for me as we go into the diner.*
*There’s a "Seat yourself!" sign up near the front, along with a stack of menus. I grab two off the top.*
Amber: "Do you need a kid's menu? With the crayons?"
*I’m just teasing her more, but her face lights up.*
Marina: "Ooh, they have those? Yeah, gimme one!"
*Mentally facepalming, I also take a coloring sheet and cup of crayons.*
*We find a booth by the window near the middle of the restaurant, between a family of four and a couple around our age. It might be the only empty spot in the whole place.*
*While I look over the breakfast specials, Marina eagerly begins to color. She’s got a dark green, two shades of red, a blue, and a yellow crayon.*
*Of course, they’re worn down to being practically round on the bottoms, which makes it hard for her to stay inside the lines.*
*When I shift my legs, I accidentally bump the table with my foot and cause her to make a large green streak across the personified eggs she was coloring.*
Amber: "Oops, sorry."
Marina: "It’s okay. What is it they say? 'There are no mistakes in art, just happy accidents?'"
Amber: "Something like that."
Amber: "Why green?"
Marina: "Do you not see what I’m coloring here, Sam-I-Amber?"
*I groan aloud at her awful joke. I’m saved from more of them by the arrival of the same waitress who greeted us. Her white uniform is stained with jelly and ketchup and who knows what else.*
Waitress: "Can I get you girls something to drink?"
Marina: "I’ll have some chocolate milk, please!"
*Her already questionable maturity suffers another blow. With the crayons in her hand, I’m not sure it can stand to take any more hits.*
Waitress: "And for you?"
Amber: "Just coffee. No cream or sugar."
Waitress: "You got it."
*Once she walks away, I nudge Marina with my foot.*
Amber: "Chocolate milk? Seriously? Are you eight?"
*Mimicking my voice, Marina shakes her shoulders.*
Marina: "Black coffee? Seriously? Are you forty?"
Amber: "Hey now, I like the taste of coffee itself."
Marina: "And I like the taste of milk itself! Just with chocolate."
Amber: "You’re a dork."
Marina: "But you love me~"
*She chimes back in a singsong voice. I know what she means, but it still causes a small lump to form in my throat. I swallow, trying to pretend that she didn’t hit a little too close to home.*
*I watch her while she goes back to coloring human/breakfast hybrids. Her lips are pursed in concentration, and there’s a little gleam of white as she bites the edge of one.*
*Seems she’s really focused. Surprisingly, she mostly manages to stay inside the lines of the pictures.*
*Rather than trying to use the dull tips, she’s switched to the flat, round ends, using the edge for more precision. It’s… Kind of working?*
*Without looking up, she starts to chat again.*
Marina: "So, where do we start looking today, Captain?"
Amber: "Oh, you finally acknowledge me as captain then, huh?"
Marina: "I figured you deserve a turn. Just this once."
Amber: "Thanks. I’ll try not to let you down."
Amber: "Anyway, I have no idea."
Marina: "You said you think it’s by one of the arches, right?"
Amber: "Well yeah. But that doesn’t really narrow it down."
*Pausing her coloring, she looks up at me.*
Marina: "It doesn’t?"
Amber: "Nah. Have you ever seen any pictures of the park?"
Marina: "A couple, I guess. I don’t know much about it though."
Amber: "Well, they named the park after the arches since there are so many of them. Probably hundreds."
Marina: "Seriously?"
Amber: "Yeah. Some are more famous than others. Let’s see…"
Amber: "There’s Windstone Arch and Double Arch; those are some of the more famous ones. You’ve probably seen pictures of those, even if you don’t know it."
Amber: "But there are a ton of lesser known ones, too. And for all we know, the treasure could be at one of them that doesn’t even have a name."
Marina: "So we’re screwed."
Amber: "Probably. But that doesn’t mean we’re giving up."
*Marina nods in satisfaction, either at my words or the slice of blue ham she just finished coloring.*
Amber: "I don’t wanna lose twice in a row. So I figure we’ll go and look around, and see if there are any clues."
Amber: "Maybe watch what other people are doing and thinking."
*Marina nods along, but she has a zoned-out look that tells me she’s not really listening. Rather, she seems to be staring past me.*
*I turn, trying to figure out what might be attracting her attention. There’s nothing except for other groups eating. I make awkward eye contact with the old lady right beside us, and I quickly face forward.*
*My words seem to break the spell.*
Amber: "You totally spaced out there."
Marina: "Oops, yeah. Sorry, I just started thinking."
*Before I can ask her what about, our waitress returns.*
*Our drinks are balanced on a tray alongside a few other guests’. Marina’s chocolate milk comes in an enormous tin cup that’s the size of my forearm. And there’s an entire pot of coffee for me.*
*She sets them down on the table between us.*
Waitress: "You girls ready to order?"
*Whoops. I haven’t looked through the menu once. I flip through it real quick, settling on a standard meal.*
Amber: "You good?"
*Marina nods.*
Amber: "I’ll have the Gentleman’s Breakfast with bacon. Eggs over-medium and sourdough toast."
Waitress: "And for you?"
Waitress: "You want the kids meal or the regular?"
*I choke on my drink as I laugh, and feel Marina’s foot crash against my leg. Whimpering at that and the dribble of coffee on my chin, I massage my shin underneath the table.*
*Marina frowns at me, and the waitress gives me a strange look. Then the typical angelic expression returns as Marina places her order.*
Marina: "The regular, please and thanks."
Waitress: "We’ll have that out for you pretty soon."
*Once again, she heads back to the kitchen, heels clicking against the tile floor.*
*When she’s out of sight, Marina begins a flurry of kicks to my shins, though these ones aren’t hard enough to hurt. Scurrying backwards in my seat, I draw my legs up closer to me.*
Marina: "Rude! Rude, rude, rude!"
Amber: "C’mon, you were asking for it with the food and the milk. The waitress asking that was just icing on the cake. The pancake."
Marina: "It’s not my fault that you sophisticated adults don’t appreciate chocolate like I do."
Amber: "You have a bigger sweet tooth than anyone I’ve ever met. Seriously, how many cavities have you had?"
Marina: "None~ I brush my teeth twice a day, every day. The last time I went to the dentist, he said my teeth were so shiny he could see his reflection in them."
Amber: "And when was that?"
*She thinks about it for a minute.*
Marina: "Maybe three years?"
Amber: "That doesn’t count anymore then."
Marina: "When was the last time that you had chocolate milk?"
*Now I have to think about that. I don’t drink a whole lot of milk to begin with, and as for chocolate…*
